Use for daily lineup optimization, start/sit calls, platoon decisions, or daily Yahoo Fantasy Baseball management. ([lyndonkl/claude](https://github.com/lyndonkl/claude/blob/HEAD/agents/mlb-lineup-optimizer.md) · ★200)
- **mlb-playoff-planner**: Plans Yahoo Fantasy Baseball playoff pushes for weeks 21-23 (ending Sep 6). From July onward, identifies players with most games and best matchups in playoff weeks, suggests trade-deadline (Aug 6) targets, evaluates IL stashes. Fires advocate (Aggressor) + critic (Stabilizer) variants. Use after July 1 for playoff positioning, trade-deadline planning, or late-season roster moves. ([lyndonkl/claude](https://github.com/lyndonkl/claude/blob/HEAD/agents/mlb-playoff-planner.md) · ★200)
- **mlb-streaming-strategist**: Plans weekly pitching moves for a Yahoo Fantasy Baseball H2H Categories league with QS/K/ERA/WHIP/SV scoring (no wins). Identifies two-start SPs, favorable spot starts, and rostered SPs to bench on bad matchups. Fires advocate (Stream) + critic (Hold) variants per candidate. Use for weekly pitching strategy, two-start pitcher targeting, spot-start streaming, or K-chasing plans. ([lyndonkl/claude](https://github.com/lyndonkl/claude/blob/HEAD/agents/mlb-streaming-strategist.md) · ★200)
- **mlb-trade-analyzer**: Evaluates Yahoo Fantasy Baseball trade offers. Runs advocate (Acceptor) + critic (Rejecter) variants, computes per-category deltas for both sides across all 10 categories (R/HR/RBI/SB/OBP, K/ERA/WHIP/QS/SV), factors in regression, positional scarcity, and playoff schedule. Verdict is ACCEPT, COUNTER (with specific counter), or REJECT. Use when a trade offer arrives, evaluating potential trade, or assessing a 2-for-1 or consolidation move. ([lyndonkl/claude](https://github.com/lyndonkl/claude/blob/HEAD/agents/mlb-trade-analyzer.md) · ★200)
- **mlb-waiver-analyst**: Scans Yahoo Fantasy Baseball free agents for weekly waiver claims (FAAB league, $100 season budget). Fires in advocate (Buy) and critic (Pass) variants, synthesizes, and produces ranked ADD + BID $X recommendations with drop candidates. Use for weekly waiver priority review, FAAB bid sizing, prospect call-ups, closer-committee speculation, or injury replacement. ([lyndonkl/claude](https://github.com/lyndonkl/claude/blob/HEAD/agents/mlb-waiver-analyst.md) · ★200)

([agentmc15/polymarket-trader](https://github.com/agentmc15/polymarket-trader/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/mm-proveout-red-team.md) · ★26)
- **mm-proveout-reviewer**: Dispatch mm-proveout-reviewer during /polytropos:execute mm-proveout at each phase boundary. ([agentmc15/polymarket-trader](https://github.com/agentmc15/polymarket-trader/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/mm-proveout-reviewer.md) · ★26)
- **mm-proveout-test-author**: Dispatch mm-proveout-test-author during /polytropos:execute mm-proveout after the implementer reports a task done and before the verifier runs, for tasks whose kit declared the `test-author` role. Writes adversarial tests derived from the task BRIEF's acceptance criteria — never from reading the implementer's code — so coverage is not circular. ([agentmc15/polymarket-trader](https://github.com/agentmc15/polymarket-trader/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/mm-proveout-test-author.md) · ★26)

([idallasjlabs/agentshroud](https://github.com/idallasjlabs/agentshroud/blob/HEAD/.omp/agents/mnemosyne.md) · 4 copies)
- **mnemosyne**: Mnemosyne — changelog keeper (titaness of memory, mother of the Muses: she holds the record). Use to update the jupiter CHANGELOG.md after a release, or to reconcile it against the git v* tags on demand. Reads git tags + release PR bodies + card refs; appends per-version feat/fix entries; never invents, never rewrites shipped entries. Read-only except the changelog file, via a draft PR. ([jellebens/gitops](https://github.com/jellebens/gitops/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/mnemosyne.md))
- **mnemosyne**: Mnemosyne (eval) — periodic eval review of the run ledgers; proposals need human sign-off. ([kalkirose/agentic-factory-olympus](https://github.com/kalkirose/agentic-factory-olympus/blob/HEAD/agents/mnemosyne.md))
- **mnemosyne**: Memory reconstruction. Turns an evidence bundle from `daedalus reconstruct gather` into a proposal of gameplan entries for a period the gameplan never recorded: status changes to open tasks, the missing phases and tasks, and roadmap items whose status the evidence contradicts. Every entry cites commits and carries a confidence label. Writes only to .daedalus/reconstruction/. Used by the /reconstruct skill; Momus checks her work before anyone sees it. ([KohlJary/daedalus](https://github.com/KohlJary/daedalus/blob/HEAD/src/daedalus/plugin/agents/mnemosyne.md))
- **mnemosyne**: Mnemosyne — the keeper of the record in the Olympus family. Commits the family's work to git and pushes it to GitHub, writing it into lasting memory. Always commits as Maleen Kidiwela and never adds a Claude co-author trailer. Reads and runs git; use it when work is ready to be committed and pushed. ([MaleenKidiwela/Olympus](https://github.com/MaleenKidiwela/Olympus/blob/HEAD/plugins/olympus/agents/mnemosyne.md))
- **mnemosyne**: Studio project ledger keeper. Query the project database for estimation comps, log a completed project, audit harvest obligations (case study, blog post, testimonial), or answer questions about studio history - sectors, stacks, margins, estimate accuracy. Athena calls her for comps and Harvest for status; Mark calls her directly after project closure or when preparing a proposal. ([markbaindesign/bain-studio](https://github.com/markbaindesign/bain-studio/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/mnemosyne.md))
- **mnemosyne**: PROACTIVELY diagnose and resolve Supabase, BullMQ, and Redis setup and runtime issues. MUST BE USED for any infrastructure setup, migration, connectivity failure, or worker debugging task involving these three services. Automatically invoke for "can't connect to", "migration failed", "jobs not processing", "Redis error", or "Supabase auth" issues. Refreshes official docs before giving guidance. Returns an actionable runbook with setup checklist, troubleshooting sequence, and recommended fix path. ([MikeBlakeway/tribes](https://github.com/MikeBlakeway/tribes/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/mnemosyne.md))
- **mnemosyne-artifact-smith**: Generates self-contained, 100% offline CSP-locked interactive HTML explorables to `/tmp/viz-output.html` enforcing the 7-Clause Explorable Contract, and registers them via `python3 scripts/mnemosyne.py artifact set --topic &lt;T&gt; --node &lt;N&gt; --file /tmp/viz-output.html`. ([zatzk/mnemosyne](https://github.com/zatzk/mnemosyne/blob/HEAD/agents/mnemosyne-artifact-smith.md))
- **mnemosyne-assessor**: Blind Zero-Context Grader for Mnemosyne. Consumes ONLY the stripped Claim-Check JSON envelope from `python3 scripts/mnemosyne.py stash export-assessor --sid &lt;SID&gt;` (plus `verify-expr` for math/procedure nodes). Never reads chat transcripts or FSRS history. Outputs a strict JSON receipt payload enforcing `RN-01`, `RN-02`, `RN-06`, and `NFR-05`. ([zatzk/mnemosyne](https://github.com/zatzk/mnemosyne/blob/HEAD/agents/mnemosyne-assessor.md))
- **mnemosyne-curriculum-architect**: Decomposes any topic into a backward-chained First-Principles Epistemic DAG (`topics/&lt;slug&gt;.json`) with Unconditional Truth roots, 3Blue1Brown Motivated Discovery derivations, co-authored probes/rubrics, and knowledge-kind routing (`concept`, `procedure`, `fact`). Validates via `python3 scripts/mnemosyne.py validate-dag` and persists atomic topic state via `add-topic`. ([zatzk/mnemosyne](https://github.com/zatzk/mnemosyne/blob/HEAD/agents/mnemosyne-curriculum-architect.md))
- **mnemosyne-mermaid-maker**: Generates high-contrast, minimal Mermaid diagrams for discrete topologies, dependency DAGs, sequence flows, and state machines. Validates syntax and rendering (up to 3 iterations), registers the Mermaid block on the node, and returns a native ```mermaid fence for Obsidian. ([zatzk/mnemosyne](https://github.com/zatzk/mnemosyne/blob/HEAD/agents/mnemosyne-mermaid-maker.md))
- **mnemosyne-opus**: Mnemosyne (eval), Opus fallback seat — periodic eval review of the run ledgers; proposals need human sign-off. ([kalkirose/agentic-factory-olympus](https://github.com/kalkirose/agentic-factory-olympus/blob/HEAD/agents/mnemosyne-opus.md))
- **mnemosyne-researcher**: Epistemological and technical fact-checking subagent. Harvests high-trust documentation, RFCs, textbooks, and primary papers in an isolated context window to verify Unconditional Truths, definitions, formulas, and documented misconceptions before curriculum design or tutoring. ([zatzk/mnemosyne](https://github.com/zatzk/mnemosyne/blob/HEAD/agents/mnemosyne-researcher.md))
- **Mnemosyne-subagent.agent**: Setup and troubleshooting specialist for Supabase, BullMQ, and Redis ([MikeBlakeway/tribes](https://github.com/MikeBlakeway/tribes/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/Mnemosyne-subagent.agent.md))
- **mnemosyne-svg-maker**: Generates clean, deterministic custom SVGs for spatial geometry, coordinate plots, vectors, and contiguous memory/byte layouts to `/tmp/viz-output.svg`, verifies rendering (up to 3 iterations), and registers via `python3 scripts/mnemosyne.py artifact set --topic &lt;T&gt; --node &lt;N&gt; --file /tmp/viz-output.svg`. ([zatzk/mnemosyne](https://github.com/zatzk/mnemosyne/blob/HEAD/agents/mnemosyne-svg-maker.md))
- **mnm-architect**: Spécialiste architecture MnM. - **mnn-source-inspector**: Use this agent to investigate Alibaba MNN internals from the actual source — architecture, public API signatures and ownership semantics, implementation details (sessions, tensors, LLM engine, kernel/backend dispatch) and the calling repository's MNN patches — in any repository that builds on or converts to MNN (e.g. SpeechLM.cpp.v3, Granite, Qwen2.5-Omni.cpp).
